## Contacts



### Phase I. North American
#### 1. North America (United States Government)

Entities within the .gov domain responsible for federal research, energy policy, and economic development.

*   **tpo@nist.gov**
    *   **Type:** Federal Technology Transfer Office
    *   **Location:** Gaithersburg, MD (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The Technology Partnerships Office (TPO) at NIST facilitates the transfer of federal technologies to the marketplace. They manage patents, licensing, and collaborative research agreements to help private industry commercialize innovations developed at NIST labs. 

*   **DOENews@hq.doe.gov**
    *   **Type:** Federal Public Affairs / Media
    *   **Location:** Washington, D.C. (USA)
    *   **Brief:** This is the primary media contact for the U.S. Department of Energy (DOE). The Office of Public Affairs handles national press inquiries, press releases, and major announcements regarding the agency's policies, research funding, and national energy initiatives. 

*   **gdo_comms@hq.doe.gov**
    *   **Type:** Federal Program Communications
    *   **Location:** Washington, D.C. (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The Grid Deployment Office (GDO) communications team manages specific infrastructure projects. They coordinate with grant recipients, utilities, and stakeholders regarding the modernization of the electric grid, transmission lines, and distribution systems. 

*   **tvainfo@tva.gov**
    *   **Type:** Federal Corporation (Utility)
    *   **Location:** Knoxville/Chattanooga, TN (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The Tennessee Valley Authority is a federally owned corporation and the nation's largest public utility. It provides electricity for 10 million people across the southeastern U.S. while also managing river navigation, flood control, and economic development. 

*   **dmped.eom@dc.gov**
    *   **Type:** Municipal Government / Economic Development
    *   **Location:** Washington, D.C. (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The Office of the Deputy Mayor for Planning and Economic Development (DMPED) is the executive agency responsible for the District of Columbia’s economic strategy. They manage major development projects, business incentives, and housing policy. 

#### 2. North America (Private Sector - Technology & Manufacturing)

Major publicly traded and private corporations involved in hardware, chips, and industrial goods.

*   **press@tsmc.com**
    *   **Type:** Corporate Communications (Semiconductors)
    *   **Location:** Hsinchu, Taiwan (Global HQ)
    *   **Brief:** Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Company (TSMC) is the world's largest dedicated semiconductor foundry. This alias routes to the Public Relations department, handling global press inquiries and official statements regarding the company's financial performance and technology roadmap. 

*   **sales_asia@tsmc.com**
    *   **Type:** Business Development (Semiconductors)
    *   **Location:** Hsinchu, Taiwan / Pan-Pacific Region
    *   **Brief:** This email is specifically for new customer engagement in the Asia-Pacific region (excluding China and Japan). It is used by original equipment manufacturers (OEMs) and fabless chip designers to initiate a foundry relationship with TSMC.

Universities and technical colleges focused on research, engineering, and regional workforce development.

*   **research@utk.edu**
    *   **Type:** University Research Administration
    *   **Location:** Knoxville, TN (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The University of Tennessee, Knoxville is the state's flagship research university. This address typically handles inquiries related to sponsored programs, compliance, and the Office of Research, Innovation & Economic Development.

*   **communications@utk.edu**
    *   **Type:** University Public Affairs
    *   **Location:** Knoxville, TN (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The central communications office for UT Knoxville manages media relations, internal announcements, and brand strategy for the university's 30,000+ students and faculty.

*   **cs-chair@cs.nyu.edu**
    *   **Type:** Academic Department Leadership
    *   **Location:** New York, NY (USA)
    *   **Brief:** The Chair of the Computer Science department at NYU's Courant Institute of Mathematical Sciences. This contact would be relevant for academic collaborations, faculty hiring, or departmental inquiries regarding their graduate and undergraduate programs.

*   **pizzini@cs.nyu.edu**
    *   **Type:** Faculty Contact
    *   **Location:** New York, NY (USA)
    *   **Brief:** Likely affiliated with the Computer Science department at NYU. The specific research focus would require additional search, but the .edu and subdomain confirm affiliation with the Courant Institute.

### Key Safety Notice

A few of the emails in the original list come from high-risk or unverifiable sources:

1.  **Kandahar@adf-af.org** - This domain format does not match any official Afghan government patterns (which use .gov.af). The "adf-af.org" domain has no official presence in defense registries.
2.  **Afghanistan (.gov.af) addresses** - The Islamic Republic of Afghanistan government collapsed in 2021. These email systems may be inactive, unmonitored, or potentially compromised.
3.  **Individual staff emails** (e.g., `meitav@idf.gov.il`, `kmurtuzov@bsec-organization.org`) - These appear to be specific to named individuals rather than functional departments. They may be out-of-date if personnel have changed roles.

### Key Research Notes on Turkish Agencies

**Domain Transition for OKA:** The Middle Black Sea Development Agency appears to have transitioned from `oka.org.tr` to `oka.gov.tr`. Your vCard contains `emre.arslanbay@oka.org.tr`, which may still function but is using the legacy domain. For future communications with OKA, the `@oka.gov.tr` domain is likely the current standard .

**TOBB's Role:** TOBB (`tobb.org.tr`) is a critical institution in Turkey's private sector. They act as an umbrella organization for all chambers of commerce and commodity exchanges. The `rusyamasasi@tobb.org.tr` email is particularly interesting as it indicates active trade facilitation with Russia despite geopolitical tensions .

**Ford's Global Contact Structure:** Ford maintains a sophisticated regional contact system for fleet and commercial customers. **IBM's Regional Support Structure:** IBM uses a regional support system with dedicated emails for North America (`customersupport.NA@ibm.com`), Latin America (`customersupport.LA@ibm.com`), Europe/Middle East/Africa (`customersupport.EMEA@ibm.com`), and Asia-Pacific (`customersupport.APAC@ibm.com`) .

**Samsung's Innovation Programs:** Samsung operates several startup and innovation programs. The `LEAPS@samsung.com` address is specifically for their LEAP-S program, which runs proof-of-concept projects with promising technology startups .

### Key Research Notes on Financial Services

**Goldman Sachs PSI Division:** The Public Sector & Infrastructure (PSI) team at Goldman Sachs is a dedicated group focused on government and public sector clients. They work on public-private partnerships (P3s), infrastructure financing, and municipal advisory services. The email `gs-psi@gs.com` is a legitimate contact point for these specialized services .

**Wells Fargo Board Communications:** The `BoardCommunications@wellsfargo.com` email is a formal channel for shareholders and stakeholders to communicate directly with Wells Fargo's Board of Directors. This is typically used for governance matters, shareholder proposals, and executive compensation inquiries. All communications are reviewed by the Office of the Corporate Secretary before being forwarded to directors .

**Two Sigma's Security Focus:** The existence of `fraud@twosigma.com` as a dedicated reporting channel indicates the firm's significant focus on cybersecurity and fraud prevention. Two Sigma is a quantitative trading firm that relies heavily on proprietary algorithms and data systems, making security a critical operational priority .

**Monzo's Press Operations:** Monzo has a dedicated press office that handles media inquiries for the UK and international markets. The bank is known for its transparent communication style and active engagement with financial journalists. For non-press inquiries, Monzo uses separate channels including `help@monzo.com` (customer support) and `partnerships@monzo.com` (business development) .

### Key Findings & Changes Since Original vCard Compilation

Several of the media emails in your vCard file appear to be **legacy contacts** that major news organizations have since replaced with more secure systems:

1. **The Washington Post deprecated `tips@washpost.com`** in favor of `lockbox@washpost.com` and encrypted channels like Signal (`wapo.1877`).

2. **Fox News maintains show-specific emails** rather than generic `comments@` addresses. The 2010 directory (the most comprehensive public listing) shows specific show emails that may still function but some shows/hosts have changed.

3. **MSNBC now uses an "Insights Community"** rather than direct `opinion@` emails for viewer feedback.

4. **NY Times and WSJ** have robust secure tip systems (Signal, SecureDrop) that they prioritize over simple email tips.

### Key Research Notes

**AGCO's Global Structure:** AGCO uses consistent `Firstname.Lastname@agcocorp.com` and `Firstname.Lastname@uk.agcocorp.com` patterns across its global operations. The company has major facilities in North America, Europe, South America, and Asia.

**Utility Interconnection Contacts:** The utility emails in this section (`customerownedgeneration@duke-energy.com`, `dgcoordinator-ohio@aep.com`, `interconnection@aes.com`, `TE_interconnection@firstenergycorp.com`) are particularly valuable for:
- Solar installers seeking grid connection approvals
- Renewable energy developers
- Battery storage project managers
- Homeowners installing rooftop solar

**John Deere's Africa Presence:** John Deere has been expanding its African operations significantly, with regional hubs in South Africa, Nigeria, Kenya, and Ghana. The `Africa@JohnDeere.com` email is a legitimate regional contact.

**⚠️ Warning:** The email `rrmia@yahoo.com` appears in the original vCard and is **not recommended** for any professional or business use. Personal email addresses (@yahoo.com, @gmail.com, @hotmail.com, etc.) should be treated with extreme caution as they may not be legitimate business contacts.

### Key Research Notes

**Latin American Media Landscape:** Many major Latin American newspapers have transitioned away from generic `redaccion@`, `contacto@`, and `info@` aliases in favor of online contact forms, departmental emails (e.g., `politica@`, `economia@`, `deportes@`), or individual reporter emails. The emails in your vCard may be **legacy contacts** that no longer receive active monitoring.

**North Korea (.kp Domain) Communications:**
- **Warning:** Any communication to `@star-co.net.kp` or `@korea-dpr.info` addresses is subject to monitoring by the North Korean government.
- These emails are likely associated with DPRK state propaganda, official portals (Naenara), or state news services (Korean Telegraph Agency).
- International sanctions may apply to certain types of communication with DPRK entities.
- Not recommended for business or journalistic purposes without specific legal guidance.

**Venezuela Media Reliability:** Due to political and economic conditions in Venezuela, many media outlets have reduced operations, changed contact information, or ceased publication. Exercise caution when attempting to contact Venezuelan media through legacy email addresses.

**Personal Email Accounts:** Several addresses in the original vCard use free email services (`@gmail.com`, `@yahoo.com`, `@yahoo.it`, `@hotmail.com`, `@cantv.net`, `@prodigy.net.mx`). These are **not recommended** for professional or business communication as they may be:
- Personal accounts of former employees
- Outdated or abandoned
- Phishing or scam attempts

This office coordinates liturgical formation, events, and resources for dioceses across Italy. The email is consistently referenced in official CEI organizational notes for event registration, payment confirmation, and general secretariat inquiries . |
| **csscuola@chiesacattolica.it** | Centro Sportivo Scolastico (School Sports Center) | Rome, Italy | This contact appears to relate to Catholic school sports programs under the CEI. Likely handles inquiries about inter-diocesan athletic events and school sports coordination. |

### A.2 Diocese of Turin (Diocesi di Torino)

The Archdiocese of Turin maintains a comprehensive set of departmental emails under the `@diocesi.to.it` domain.

| Email Address | Department/Office | Brief |
|---|---|---|
| **centralino@diocesi.to.it** | Main Switchboard / General Inquiries | Primary contact for the Archdiocese of Turin's central offices. Routes calls and general correspondence. |
| **catechistico@diocesi.to.it** | Catechetical Office | Handles religious education programs, catechist training, and sacramental preparation for children and adults. |
| **catecumenato@diocesi.to.it** | Catechumenate Office | Focuses on the Rite of Christian Initiation for Adults (RCIA), welcoming new converts to the Catholic faith. |
| **liturgico@diocesi.to.it** | Liturgical Office | Coordinates liturgical celebrations, training for liturgical ministers, and guidance on proper liturgical practice within the diocese. |
| **pastorale.scolastica@diocesi.to.it** | School Pastoral Office | Manages religious education and pastoral care within Catholic schools across the Archdiocese of Turin. |
| **irc.scuola@diocesi.to.it** | Religious Instruction in Schools | Specifically handles the teaching of Catholic religion in public and private schools (IRC = Insegnamento della Religione Cattolica). |
| **cultura@diocesi.to.it** | Culture Office | Promotes cultural events, art, and heritage within the diocese. Engages with museums, archives, and cultural institutions. |
| **missionario@diocesi.to.it** | Missionary Office | Coordinates missionary activity, supports missionaries abroad, and fosters mission awareness within the diocese. |
| **collette.donazioni@diocesi.to.it** | Donations & Collections | Handles financial contributions, special collections, and donation processing for diocesan initiatives. |
| **giovani@diocesi.to.it** | Youth Ministry | Manages programs for young adults, youth groups, retreats, and World Youth Day coordination. |
| **famiglia@diocesi.to.it** | Family Ministry | Supports marriage preparation, family counseling, and initiatives for families within the diocese. |
| **caritas@diocesi.to.it** | Caritas (Charitable Arm) | Coordinates charitable works, food banks, shelter programs, and assistance for the poor and marginalized. |
| **migranti@diocesi.to.it** | Migrant & Refugee Office | Assists migrants, refugees, and displaced persons with integration, legal support, and pastoral care. |
| **lavoro@diocesi.to.it** | Labor / Workplace Ministry | Addresses issues of work, unemployment, labor rights, and chaplaincy for workers. |
| **salute@diocesi.to.it** | Health & Hospital Chaplaincy | Coordinates pastoral care in hospitals, nursing homes, and healthcare facilities. |
| **cancelleria@diocesi.to.it** | Chancery Office | The administrative hub of the diocese, handling official documents, canonical records, and diocesan archives. |
| **sacramenti@diocesi.to.it** | Sacraments Office | Manages the administration of sacraments (Baptism, Confirmation, Marriage, Holy Orders) at the diocesan level. |
| **protocollo.matrimoni@diocesi.to.it** | Marriage Protocol | Specialized office for marriage documentation, prenuptial inquiries, and canonical marriage records. |

## Key Research Notes

**Italian Church Email Patterns:** The Italian Catholic Church extensively uses the `@chiesacattolica.it` domain for national offices (like ULN) and individual dioceses use patterns like `@diocesi.to.it` (Turin), `@diocesi.trieste.it`, etc. However, **many Italian parishes and smaller dioceses still rely on free email services** (Gmail, Libero, Tiscali, Tin.it), which should be treated with caution .

**Irish Parish Communications:** The Diocese of Kerry publishes parish emails directly in weekly bulletins, confirming `castleisland@dioceseofkerry.ie` as actively monitored . However, many smaller Irish parishes use free Gmail addresses.

**Saskatoon Diocese (Canada):** The Roman Catholic Diocese of Saskatoon has a well-organized digital presence with verified functional emails for Communications, Chancellor's Office, and Safeguarding .

**Spanish "Planalfa.es" Domain:** Many Spanish dioceses appear to use `@planalfa.es` as a centralized hosting service. While these follow official naming patterns, the domain itself is not obviously a church domain (Planalfa appears to be a Spanish IT services company). Use with appropriate caution.

**Caritas Mallorca (Spain):** Highly verified charitable organization with strong documentation of their work .
Here is **Phase 11** of the reorganized directory, focusing on Healthcare & Medical Institutions.

## Section A: Cuban Healthcare System (Sistema Nacional de Salud de Cuba)

Cuba operates a centralized, government-run healthcare system under the Ministry of Public Health (MINSAP). Domains include `@infomed.sld.cu` (national health network), `@sld.cu`, and various hospital-specific domains.

The `@infomed.sld.cu` domain is Cuba's national health information network. |
| **director.trinidad@smcsalud.cu** | Trinidad Health Center | Trinidad, Sancti Spíritus | **Director's office at the Trinidad health facility.** `smcsalud.cu` appears to be a regional health network domain. Trinidad is a major tourist destination with a historic colonial center. |

## Key Research Notes

**Cuban Healthcare System (`@infomed.sld.cu`):** The `infomed.sld.cu` domain is the official health information network of Cuba's Ministry of Public Health (MINSAP). It serves as the national platform for healthcare communication, telemedicine, and research. Emails following `*@infomed.sld.cu` patterns are legitimate, though direct public verification of individual aliases is limited due to Cuba's restricted internet infrastructure.

**Taiwanese Healthcare System (`@mohw.gov.tw`):** Taiwan's Ministry of Health and Welfare oversees a network of regional hospitals. The `ethics@` and `security@` emails follow a standard naming pattern for hospital ethics committees and security offices. The `@mohw.gov.tw` domain is the official government health domain.

**German University Hospitals:** Heidelberg University Hospital (`med.uni-heidelberg.de`) and University Hospital Cologne (`uk-koeln.de`) are both highly reputable, state-of-the-art medical centers. Their email patterns follow standard German academic medical conventions.

**Chile's HCUCH:** Hospital Clínico Universidad Católica is consistently ranked among the best hospitals in Latin America. The `@hcuch.cl` domain is the official hospital domain.

**Warning on Free Emails:** Some healthcare contacts in the original vCard (e.g., `hospitaloncologicopadremachadoi@gmail.com` in Peru) use free Gmail addresses. While the institution may be legitimate, the use of personal email domains suggests limited IT infrastructure and should be treated with appropriate caution.

| **dppa-scsb3@un.org** | Security Council Secretariat Branch (Verified) | United Nations, New York | **Official contact for the UN Security Council Secretariat Branch** . This email handles logistical and procedural matters for Council members, including requests to circulate documents as official Security Council records, communications to the Council presidency, and submissions from Member States who are not Council members. The address is published in official UN documentation for formal correspondence with the Security Council Affairs Division (SCAD) of the Department of Political and Peacebuilding Affairs (DPPA) . |

## Key Research Notes

**UN Security Council Contact (`dppa-scsb3@un.org`):** This is the official UN email for the Security Council Secretariat Branch. It is cited in multiple UN documents for official correspondence regarding Council meetings, document circulation, and non-member state participation .

**China's MOD Contact (`mod@mod.gov.cn`):** Confirmed on both the Chinese and English versions of the Ministry of National Defense website as the official contact email. The domain `mod.gov.cn` is the authoritative defense ministry domain .

**Israel MOD Contact (`pniot@mod.gov.il`):** Verified in Israel's official Freedom of Information Act reports from 2011 and 2013. The address is listed under "Citizen Service/Public Inquiries" and is still referenced in current government directories .

**Russian Government Email System (`gov.ru`, `duma.gov.ru`, etc.):** All domains are legitimate Russian government domains. However, following the 2022 invasion of Ukraine and subsequent international sanctions, many Russian government email systems have reduced international accessibility. Individual staff emails (`PolyakovAV@economy.gov.ru`, `DeinovIV@economy.gov.ru`) are especially likely to be outdated.

**DPRK Mission to the UN (`mission.korea-dpr@ties.itu.int`):** Verified in official ITU documentation for the 2022 Plenipotentiary Conference alongside other member state missions (e.g., ROK Mission: `geneva.korea@mofa.go.kr`) . Communication with this address is official diplomatic correspondence via the ITU system and is subject to DPRK government monitoring.

**Pakistan MOFA (`mofa.gov.pk`):** The `mofa.gov.pk` domain is Pakistan's official Ministry of Foreign Affairs domain. Contacts from this domain are legitimate, though the specific repatriation cell addresses may be inactive following the situation in Afghanistan.

**Afghanistan Warning:** The Islamic Republic of Afghanistan government collapsed in August 2021. Any `@mfa.af`, `@gov.af`, or `@arzaq.gov.af` addresses are likely defunct, unmonitored, or under Taliban control. The `adf-af.org` address does not match official Afghan government patterns and should be treated as potentially malicious.

## Key Research Notes

**Fully Verified Contacts:**

1. **`uln@chiesacattolica.it` (Italy CEI):** This email is extensively documented in official CEI organizational notes from 2022-2025. The secretariat staff (Ornella Russo, Erasto Trujillo) and phone numbers (0666398.234/216) are consistently cited .

2. **`caritas@caritasmallorca.org` (Spain):** Confirmed through official Caritas press releases and annual reports. Caritas España invested 486.5 million euros in 2023, supporting over 2.5 million people .

3. **`castleisland@dioceseofkerry.ie` (Ireland):** Consistently published in weekly parish bulletins from 2021 through 2025. The parish priest Fr. Maurice Brick is the verified contact .

4. **Archdiocese of Madrid (`@archidiocesis.madrid`):** Official geo-TLD domain for the Archdiocese of Madrid. Multiple departmental and parish emails follow standard patterns.

5. **Diocese of Paris (`@diocese-paris.net`):** Official domain for the Archdiocese of Paris, with verified departmental emails for communications, catechumenate, and data protection.

**Pattern Verified Domains:**

- **`@diocesi.to.it` (Turin):** Official domain of the Archdiocese of Turin. Departmental emails follow standard Italian naming conventions.
- **`@diocesi.trieste.it`, `@diocesipadova.it`, `@diocesicrema.it`, etc.:** All are official Italian diocesan domains under the CEI system.
- **`@planalfa.es` (Spanish dioceses):** A centralized hosting service used by multiple Spanish dioceses. While the domain is not an obvious church domain, it has been used consistently for official communications.
- **`@rcdos.ca` (Saskatoon), `@dol.ca` (London, ON), `@diocesemontreal.org` (Montreal):** Official Canadian diocesan domains.

**Exercise Caution With:**
- **Free email services** (Gmail, Yahoo, Hotmail, Libero, Tiscali) - Not official church communications channels.
- **ISP emails** (tin.it, tiscali.it, alice.it, wanadoo.fr, kpnmail.nl, sasktel.net, mymts.net, bell.net) - Personal accounts of individuals, may be outdated.
- **Individual staff emails** (`r.gulino@diocesifirenze.it`, etc.) - Personnel may have changed roles or left.
- **Defunct ISP domains** (wanadoo.fr, alice.it) - Likely inactive.

## Key Findings & Recommendations

### Critical Warnings

1. **`aaaaaaazzzzz.09.852@gmail.com`** - This email address exhibits a pattern consistent with automated account creation, spam bots, or temporary/disposable email addresses. **Do not use this address for any purpose.**

2. **QQ and 163.com addresses** - Chinese email services are frequently associated with spam, phishing attempts, and automated bot activity. Numeric-only QQ addresses are particularly high-risk.

3. **ISP email addresses** (`@tin.it`, `@tiscali.it`, `@alice.it`, `@wanadoo.fr`, `@eircom.net`, `@bigpond.com`, etc.) - These are personal accounts provided by internet service providers. Many of these ISPs no longer exist (Alice Italy, Wanadoo) or have been rebranded. These addresses are likely inactive or abandoned.

### Church & Religious Organization Pattern

Many smaller Catholic parishes, particularly in Ireland, Italy, Spain, and Canada, use personal email services (Gmail, Yahoo, Libero) rather than official diocesan domains. This is common for smaller parishes with limited IT resources but should be treated with appropriate caution.

### Geographic Concentration

- **Italy:** Highest concentration of ISP/free email addresses (Libero, Tiscali, Tin.it, Alice.it, Inwind.it)
- **Ireland:** High concentration of parish Gmail addresses
- **Canada:** Mix of Gmail and ISP (MTS, SaskTel, Bell) addresses
- **China:** QQ and 163.com addresses (high spam risk)

### Recommendations for Use

| If you are... | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| A business professional | **Do not use** any addresses from Phase 14 |
| A researcher cataloging contacts | Document as "unverified personal" but do not rely on |
| A marketer building a list | **Do not use** - high bounce/spam complaint risk |
| A journalist seeking sources | Find official domain alternatives first |
| Anyone sending sensitive information | **Never use** - no security or verification |

## Key Research Notes

**BairesDev (`info@bairesdev.com`):** 
This is a highly successful bootstrapped company with verified metrics including Financial Times recognition for 764% revenue growth . They serve major enterprise clients including Google and Johnson & Johnson . The `info@` alias is a legitimate general contact channel for their business development team.

**ioet (`info@ioet.com`):**
Founded in Silicon Valley in 2014, ioet operates as a nearshore development provider with teams across Latin America . The company has over 160 software professionals and serves clients from startups to Fortune 500 companies . The `info@` email is their primary general inquiry contact.

**Startup Email Patterns:**
Both `hi@descasio.io` and `hello@qubika.com` follow modern startup conventions (using friendly greetings as email aliases). This suggests these are likely active companies with contemporary branding, though specific public information is limited.

# RECOMMENDATIONS

## Verified Contacts (✅)
**Recommended for professional use.** These have been confirmed through official government websites, corporate press releases, or published directories.

## Probable Contacts (○)
**Use with normal caution.** These use official domain patterns and are likely active, but individual alias verification was not possible in this research.

## Unverified / Personal (⚠️)
**NOT recommended for business use.** These include Gmail, Yahoo, Hotmail, ISP, and free email services. May be personal accounts, outdated, or associated with spam.

## Critical Warning (🔴)
**DO NOT USE.** These include:
- DPRK (North Korea) contacts – communication monitored, sanctions implications
- Afghanistan former government contacts – likely defunct or compromised
- `Kandahar@adf-af.org` – potential fraud
- `aaaaaaazzzzz.09.852@gmail.com` – likely bot/spam
